# Changes from the original project

This release was exported from the project folder in which the studies ran. Files are copied byte for byte
except those listed below. There are three kinds of change:

- **adapted:** code whose paths pointed into other folders on the original machine (model checkpoints, the
  environment of a sibling project, the original workspace's Modal app names) now uses `MODELS_DIR` (default
  `models/`), paths inside this release, or environment variables; and the verifiers' frozen-file checks accept
  the documented changes listed here (`tools/release_paths.py`). The reasons column says which.
- **redacted:** records in which personal or machine-specific strings were replaced: absolute paths and
  sibling-project folder names by placeholders (`<project>`, `<portfolio>`, `<models>`, `<environment>`,
  `<environment-project>`, `<sibling-project>`, `<home>`), the operating-system name in platform strings by
  `[distro]`, account identifiers of cloud objects by `[redacted]`, and the names of the writing and coding tools in working records by the placeholder `[redacted]` (the assistants used are named in README.md, under "Use of AI assistants"). Nothing else in these files changed; the counts show how many strings of each
  kind were replaced.
- **not shipped:** files named in a freeze record but left out (logs, the original project's provenance notes,
  and the environment files of the original runs, which name the original project; `pyproject.toml` and
  `uv.lock` here pin the same package versions).

Inputs larger than 1 MB are shipped compressed (`.xz`) and saved outputs as archives under
`data/saved-outputs/`; `tools/unpack.py` restores them byte for byte and checks each SHA-256 against
`data/PACKED-FILES.json`. Compression is not a change and is not listed below.

To check every freeze record against this release: `make check-freezes`.
